The root comes last.\nfunc (validator JwtX509Validator) checkCertRevocation(verifiedChain []*x509.Certificate) error {\n\tfor i, certToCheck := range verifiedChain {\n\t\t// issuer is normally the next cert in the chain, except for the root which is self signed\n\t\tissuerIdx := 1 + i\n\t\tif issuerIdx == len(verifiedChain) {\n\t\t\tissuerIdx = i\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tfor _, crlPoint := range certToCheck.CRLDistributionPoints {\n\t\t\tcrl, err := validator.crls.GetCRL(crlPoint)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"unable to get the crl for cert '%s': %w\", certToCheck.Subject.String(), err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif crl.HasExpired(nowFunc()) {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"crl has expired since: %s\", crl.TBSCertList.NextUpdate.String())","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":260,"totalLines":274,"truncated":true}},"title":"auth/services/x509/x509_validator.go","time":{"start":1782067898872,"end":1782067898970}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_009aa331be36108b016a3832ba93988197a49fcd25db8d5f95"}},"id":"prt_eeb8629f0001IknQjAuf1Tkvbi","sessionID":"ses_1147a0b65ffeMM07blELOeVZAA","messageID":"msg_eeb861aac001kewPsLZszOEqA1"}}

{\n\theaders := jws.NewHeaders()\n\tfor key, value := range protectedHeaders {\n\t\tif err := headers.Set(key, value); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"unable to set header %s: %w\", key, peerIDFromMetadata(md)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tpeer := Peer{\n\t\tID:      peerID,\n\t\tAddress: peerCtx.Addr.String(),\n\t}\n\tlog.Logger().Infof(\"New peer connected (peer=%s)\", peer)\n\t// We received our peer's PeerID, now send our own.\n\tif err := stream.SendHeader(constructMetadata(n.config.PeerID)); err != nil {\n\t\treturn errors2.Wrap(err, \"unable to send headers\")\n\t}\n\tn.acceptPeer(peer, stream)\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// acceptPeer registers a connection, associating the gRPC stream with the given peer. It starts the goroutines required\n// for receiving and sending messages from/to the peer. It should be called from the gRPC service handler,\n// so when this function exits (and the service handler as well), goroutines spawned for calling Recv() will exit.\nfunc (n *adapter) acceptPeer(peer Peer, stream grpcMessenger) {\n\tconn := n.conns.register(peer, stream)\n\tn.peerConnectedChannel <- conn.peer()\n\tconn.exchange(n.receivedMessages)\n\t// Previous call was blocking, if we reach this the connection has either errored out, been disconnected\n\t// by the local node or by the peer. We still need to explicitly close it to clean up the connection\n\t// (in case it's closed due to a network error or by the peer).\n\tn.conns.close(peer.ID)\n\tn.peerDisconnectedChannel <- peer\n}","lineStart":260,"lineEnd":403,"totalLines":403,"truncated":false}},"title":"network/p2p/impl.go","time":{"start":1782068122909,"end":1782068122935}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_0890f6bf89617765016a38339a8de48196b7dced59797b6c88"}},"id":"prt_eeb899517001QaIBCJTKt5q4wR","sessionID":"ses_1147a0b65ffeMM07blELOeVZAA","messageID":"msg_eeb897e2e0013sF3ehV108V50R"}}
